# The preprint's figure check
Study: ed-decision-model (open medical typed-decision encoder built on Laya)   Prepared: 3 October 2026
Sources: docs/preprint/fiorillo_preprint.tex and the table fragments it inputs (docs/preprint/tables/); docs/preprint/figure_sources.json (written 2026-10-03T12:16:31Z); the results files it names; src/eval/check_preprint_session10.py, which wrote this file at 2026-10-03T23:59:26Z
Status: complete (the check PASSES)

Abbreviations: UTC is Coordinated Universal Time; EI is Evidence Inference 2.0; DOI is digital object identifier.

## How the check works

The check removes the LaTeX source's commands, comments, labels, citation keys, cross-references, URLs and DOIs, system names in typewriter type and the reference list, and reads every remaining number, date and time in each section (the source marks each with a line "% SECTION: <name>") and in each table fragment. Names and identifiers that contain digits (model names, versions, K1 to K10, H1 to H4, the register's row numbers, F1, the sessions' and corrections' numbers) are not numbers and are removed first; the patterns are NAMES in the script. A number PASSES when figure_sources.json registers a value printed exactly so for that section or table, and that value, read again from its file and key or computed again by this script from its files, still prints so. MISMATCH is a number whose registered value does not check. NO SOURCE is a number that comes from no results file; figure_sources.json's origins name where each comes from. Every value figure_sources.json registers for a table must also be among the numbers read from that table's fragment, so that a number the check failed to read cannot go unnoticed. The check passes with no MISMATCH, every NO SOURCE row with an origin, every registered value checking, every table read in full, and every results file unchanged since figure_sources.json was written.
